# Source conventions

Host-written times are host wall-clock time unless they carry an explicit zone. Zone, locale and score scale follow the host inventory entry for the ISO week (UTC) of the job's submission instant, and every row of a job uses that entry. Offsets during the window: UTC +00:00, Europe/Berlin +02:00, America/Chicago -05:00, Asia/Kolkata +05:30, America/Sao_Paulo -03:00. Clock offsets are host clock minus true time in milliseconds; subtract them from host-written times.

Number formats by locale: en_US 1,234.50; de_DE 1.234,50; fr_FR 1 234,50; en_IN 12,34,567.50. Documented precisions: arithmetic answers 2 decimals, safety scores 3 decimals, confidences 4 decimals, contamination overlaps 4 decimals. A pct scale divides by 100 (precision drops by 2 decimals in the written unit).

Parquet row groups hold 1000 rows each, numbered from 0.

legacy_results.txt is fixed width. Layout A: RID columns 1-9, JOB 10-19, ITEM 20-30, ANSWER 31-50 (right-aligned), EMIT 51-65 (right-aligned, YYYYMMDDHHMMSS), then a space and HOST. Layout B: RID 1-9, HOST 10-20, JOB 21-30, POS 31-33 (right-aligned), EMIT 34-48, ANSWER 49-68.

suite_manifest.xlsx sheets hold these suites: core (arith, intent), reasoning (mcq, longctx), safety_code_pref (safety, code, pref), multilingual (multiling).

scores_shard1.parquet: row groups 7, 11 store emitted as epoch seconds.

scores_shard2.parquet: row groups 5, 7 store emitted as epoch seconds.

scores_staging.parquet: row groups 2, 10 store emitted as epoch seconds.
## Per-source notes

baseline_reference.csv:
- A second header row in baseline_reference.csv swaps the value and confidence columns for the rows after it.
- Baseline rows revised before 2025-08-01 were exported from a de_DE workstation and use a comma decimal mark.
- When baseline_reference.csv holds several rows for one item, the row with the latest revised date wins.
- A baseline arithmetic value of N/A is an abstention.
- Some revised values in baseline_reference.csv are spreadsheet serial dates (days since 1899-12-30).

certification_policy.md:
- Some cells in certification_policy.md tables are wrapped in double quotes; the quotes are not part of the value.
- A table row repeated verbatim in certification_policy.md is one row.
- Pipes inside table cells are escaped as \|.
- Scope rows for unregistered items are ignored.
- A continuation table in certification_policy.md repeats the header with its columns reordered.
- The first cell of some certification_policy.md table rows ends with a no-break space.
- Where a table in certification_policy.md lists a key twice, the row with the higher revision wins.
- Revision values in certification_policy.md can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in certification_policy.md can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Cells of certification_policy.md can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

checkpoint_lineage.json:
- registered_at is ISO 8601 UTC or epoch seconds.
- A parent of null or empty string marks the first checkpoint of a training run.
- A checkpoint registered more than once keeps the entry with the latest registered_at.
- Run names can carry trailing spaces.
- step can be written in thousands with a k suffix (12.5k).

clock_offsets.txt:
- Offsets of several minutes are real (drifting hosts) and are applied.
- Offsets under one second still apply; they can move a row across a seal or window boundary.
- A #COLUMNS record changes the column order for the lines after it.
- Negative offsets can be written in parentheses.
- Positive offsets can carry an explicit + sign.
- SEQ can be zero-padded.
- OFFSET_MS is host clock minus true time; subtract it from host-written timestamps.
- A later measurement line for the same host-week (higher seq) replaces earlier ones.
- Lines can end with trailing spaces.
- UNIT can be lower case (ms, s).
- Lines with unit S give the offset in seconds with three decimals.
- The week column is the ISO week number in 2025; some lines write it as W31.
- The week can be written 2025-W31.
- Host-weeks with no line have offset 0; a line with offset ------ is a failed measurement and also means 0.

code_samples.jsonl:
- A code_samples.jsonl ts with a Z suffix was stamped by the collector in true UTC and takes no clock correction.
- In code_samples.jsonl a numeric ts is epoch milliseconds of the worker clock; a string ts is ISO 8601 with the worker's numeric offset.
- Result rows in code_samples.jsonl are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- When code_samples.jsonl holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- A code sample with exit 137 and passed -1 is a sandbox kill; it is void and the retry row for the same sample stands.
- Host-written timestamps in code_samples.jsonl carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.

contamination_hits.jsonl:
- Detector names can be upper-cased.
- Older scanner builds name the item field item_ref instead of item.
- Scans by detector ngram-v1 report overlap as a percentage (0 to 100).
- scanned_at is ISO 8601 UTC, or epoch seconds for older scanner builds.
- Some overlaps are JSON strings rather than numbers.

context_lengths.csv:
- context_lengths.csv starts with a byte order mark.
- Token counts can carry thousands separators.
- A repeated header inside context_lengths.csv reorders its columns for the rows after it.
- When context_lengths.csv holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in context_lengths.csv can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in context_lengths.csv can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Token counts from the Paris counter use a space as thousands separator.
- Counts can carry a tok suffix (1234 tok).
- Cells of context_lengths.csv can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

control_totals.html:
- Some cells in control_totals.html tables are wrapped in double quotes; the quotes are not part of the value.
- Cells use HTML entities.
- A continuation table in control_totals.html repeats the header with its columns reordered.
- The first cell of some control_totals.html table rows ends with a no-break space.
- Revision values in control_totals.html can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in control_totals.html can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- value_sum is written with en_US grouping; the de_DE desk's rows use dot grouping and a comma decimal mark.
- Cells of control_totals.html can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

dedup_clusters.tsv:
- A repeated header inside dedup_clusters.tsv reorders its columns for the rows after it.
- When dedup_clusters.tsv holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in dedup_clusters.tsv can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in dedup_clusters.tsv can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Similarity values written with a comma decimal mark come from the de_DE clustering run.
- Similarity values above 1 are percentages.
- Some dedup rows end with a trailing tab (empty fifth column).
- Cells of dedup_clusters.tsv can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

errata_register.docx:
- logged dates are day/month/year.
- Cells can end with a carriage return and line feed; strip them.
- Long targets wrap inside the table cell with a line break.

eval_store.db:
- Result rows in eval_store.db are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- emitted in eval_store.db is UTC written without a zone designator.
- When eval_store.db holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- Rows of eval_store.db for a job whose routing entry names neither eval_store.db as primary nor as copy source are stray and are ignored.

eval_store_mirror.db:
- The replica names the value column result_value; it is the same field as value in the primary.
- emitted in eval_store_mirror.db is UTC written without a zone designator.

evalops_notifications.mbox:
- Some Date headers use a zone name (CEST, CDT, IST) after the numeric offset; the offset decides.
- Body lines starting with From are escaped with >.
- Message-ID values are informational.
- Some bodies are quoted-printable (Content-Transfer-Encoding: quoted-printable); decode before reading.
- A RESEAL receipt replaces the earlier seal of the same job (the latest RESEAL Date is the seal time).
- A seal receipt fixes the job's seal time at the message Date; result rows emitted after it are ignored.
- Subject keywords can be in any case.
- Message Date headers carry the sender's numeric offset; compare instants in UTC (Europe/Berlin senders write +0200).
- Date headers from the Chicago desk carry -0500.
- Date headers from the Bengaluru desk carry +0530.

field_precedence.html:
- Some cells in field_precedence.html tables are wrapped in double quotes; the quotes are not part of the value.
- Cells use HTML entities.
- A continuation table in field_precedence.html repeats the header with its columns reordered.
- The first cell of some field_precedence.html table rows ends with a no-break space.
- Revision values in field_precedence.html can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in field_precedence.html can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Cells of field_precedence.html can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

gold_labels.tsv:
- A gold value corrected by an upheld grader erratum is replaced by the erratum's value; withdrawn errata change nothing.
- A repeated header line inside gold_labels.tsv renames gold to answer for the rows after it.
- Rows of gold_labels.tsv that are not valid UTF-8 are Latin-1.
- When gold_labels.tsv holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in gold_labels.tsv can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in gold_labels.tsv can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Cells of gold_labels.tsv can carry trailing spaces; strip them.
- Intent gold labels are written in the class names of the item's taxonomy version from the item registry.

grader_errata.xml:
- Attribute order varies between entries.
- Erratum notes are CDATA sections and can contain angle brackets.
- decided values carry numeric offsets.
- decided can be epoch seconds.
- Entries in the second block use the element correction with attribute fix instead of erratum and value.
- value attributes can carry surrounding spaces.

harness_events.log:
- Lines whose timestamp ends in Z were written by the scheduler in UTC with a corrected clock.
- Zone and clock settings for harness_events.log rows follow the inventory snapshot of the submission week, not the host's first or most common snapshot; re-imaged hosts change zone mid-quarter.
- A job can have several SUBMIT lines (queue retries); the submission instant is the earliest one after zone and clock correction.
- Host-written timestamps in harness_events.log carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.
- Timestamps in harness_events.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Europe/Berlin the offset during the window is UTC+02:00.
- Timestamps in harness_events.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Chicago the offset during the window is UTC-05:00.
- Timestamps in harness_events.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Asia/Kolkata the offset during the window is UTC+05:30.
- Timestamps in harness_events.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Sao_Paulo the offset during the window is UTC-03:00.

host_inventory.ini:
- Europe/Berlin and America/Chicago are on summer time for the whole window; there is no transition inside it.
- Unknown keys (rack=...) are ignored.
- A ; after a value starts an inline comment.
- Keys inside an entry can appear in any order.
- Locales can be written with - instead of _ and in any case.
- Hosts re-imaged mid-quarter change zone between weeks; each week uses its own entry.
- scale is unit or pct; 1 means unit and 100 means pct.
- A host-week can be listed more than once; the entry in the later-numbered snapshot section wins.
- Values can carry trailing spaces before the next separator.
- Zones can be written as abbreviations: CEST is Europe/Berlin, CDT is America/Chicago, IST is Asia/Kolkata, BRT is America/Sao_Paulo.
- The week key is ISO year-week (2025-W31); some entries write the Monday date instead.

intent_predictions.csv:
- intent_predictions.csv starts with a UTF-8 byte order mark.
- Labels are matched exactly as written, including spaces and punctuation; a label that looks like two words is still one alias.
- Raw labels resolve under the taxonomy version of the job's harness.
- intent_predictions.csv labels Europe/Berlin rows with utc_offset +0100 all year; during the window the true offset is +0200. Other offsets are correct.
- A second header row inside intent_predictions.csv renames label to predicted_label and local_time to emitted_local and moves utc_offset to the last column; rows after it follow the new header.
- Result rows in intent_predictions.csv are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- When intent_predictions.csv holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- local times in intent_predictions.csv carry the host clock error of the submission week.

item_registry.csv:
- Some added values in item_registry.csv are spreadsheet serial dates (days since 1899-12-30).
- item_registry.csv starts with a byte order mark.
- A quoted subtask containing a comma is one value.
- A second header row inside item_registry.csv swaps the subtask and suite columns for the rows after it.
- When item_registry.csv holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in item_registry.csv can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in item_registry.csv can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- A taxonomy value of - means not applicable (non-intent suite); an empty taxonomy on an intent item means v1.
- For intent items the taxonomy column says whether the item's gold label is written in taxonomy v1 or v2 class names.

job_configs.toml:
- A # after a value starts a comment.
- submitted_local can be a quoted string instead of a TOML date.
- Some job tables are written as inline tables.
- samples can be a string.
- A job configured twice keeps the table with the higher revision.
- revision can be a string with an r prefix.
- A temperature of -1 means the harness default (0.0, or 0.8 for code).
- submitted_local is the host-local submission date and is informational; the harness events give the submission instant.
- temperature can be a string.

judge_verdicts.jsonl:
- Result rows in judge_verdicts.jsonl are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- When judge_verdicts.jsonl holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- judge_verdicts.jsonl timestamps are judge-service UTC; the judge service has no host clock offset.

judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl:
- Rescored timestamps are epoch seconds.

label_aliases.json:
- Raw aliases may use JSON unicode escapes.
- Raw aliases containing / can be written with the JSON escape \/.
- Keys inside an entry can appear in any order.
- Some entries carry the class index as a string field idx; it is informational.
- When an alias appears in several entries, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- revision can be zero-padded as a string ("03").
- revision can be a string with an r prefix.

legacy_results.txt:
- Result rows in legacy_results.txt are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- A #LAYOUT B record switches legacy_results.txt to the reordered layout in which the item is given as its ordinal inside the job's shard; #LAYOUT A switches back.
- Numbers in legacy_results.txt follow the host locale of the submission week; de_DE hosts write a comma decimal mark and dot thousands grouping.
- Numbers in legacy_results.txt follow the host locale of the submission week; fr_FR hosts write a comma decimal mark and space thousands grouping.
- Numbers in legacy_results.txt follow the host locale of the submission week; en_IN hosts write Indian lakh grouping (12,34,567.50).
- Negative answers in legacy_results.txt can be written in parentheses.
- legacy_results.txt times were clock-corrected by the legacy uploader; applying the host clock offset again double-corrects them.
- When legacy_results.txt holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- In legacy_results.txt an answer of N/A is an abstention and scores as wrong.
- EMIT in legacy_results.txt is host local wall-clock time; for Europe/Berlin hosts (submission-week inventory) the offset is UTC+02:00.
- EMIT in legacy_results.txt is host local wall-clock time; for America/Chicago hosts (submission-week inventory) the offset is UTC-05:00.
- EMIT in legacy_results.txt is host local wall-clock time; for Asia/Kolkata hosts (submission-week inventory) the offset is UTC+05:30.
- EMIT in legacy_results.txt is host local wall-clock time; for America/Sao_Paulo hosts (submission-week inventory) the offset is UTC-03:00.

maintenance_windows.ics:
- The host is in X-HOST; LOCATION is the data-centre room and is not a host.
- DESCRIPTION text can mention other hosts; only X-HOST names the host.
- Some events give DURATION (PT3H) instead of DTEND.
- Long SUMMARY lines are folded (continuation lines start with a space).
- When an event UID appears more than once, the copy with the highest SEQUENCE wins.
- An event without SEQUENCE has sequence 0.
- TZID parameter values can be double-quoted.
- DTSTART/DTEND with a TZID parameter are local times in that zone; values ending in Z are UTC.
- UIDs compare case-insensitively.
- UIDs can carry an @heron.example suffix.
- Some events write X-HOST with a parameter (X-HOST;ROLE=primary:node-0001).

manual_overrides.xlsx:
- Cells can end with a carriage return and line feed; strip them.
- reviewed_at is a spreadsheet serial date.

methodology_appendix.pdf:
- Some cells in methodology_appendix.pdf tables are wrapped in double quotes; the quotes are not part of the value.
- A continuation table in methodology_appendix.pdf repeats the header with its columns reordered.
- The first cell of some methodology_appendix.pdf table rows ends with a no-break space.
- Revision values in methodology_appendix.pdf can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in methodology_appendix.pdf can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- A table row too long for the page continues on the next text line; a line ending in a backslash is joined with the line after it.
- Cells of methodology_appendix.pdf can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

metric_definitions.pdf:
- Some cells in metric_definitions.pdf tables are wrapped in double quotes; the quotes are not part of the value.
- A continuation table in metric_definitions.pdf repeats the header with its columns reordered.
- The first cell of some metric_definitions.pdf table rows ends with a no-break space.
- Revision values in metric_definitions.pdf can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in metric_definitions.pdf can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- A table row too long for the page continues on the next text line; a line ending in a backslash is joined with the line after it.
- Cells of metric_definitions.pdf can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

model_tags.yaml:
- Event at values carry a numeric offset; the registry ran on UTC+05:30 for part of the quarter.
- Tag names that would parse as YAML scalars of another type are quoted.

prompt_locales.tsv:
- A repeated header inside prompt_locales.tsv reorders its columns for the rows after it.
- When prompt_locales.tsv holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in prompt_locales.tsv can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in prompt_locales.tsv can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Cells of prompt_locales.tsv can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

rerun_results.jsonl:
- Arithmetic rerun answers are strings in plain en_US form with no grouping; a leading minus is a negative answer.
- Multiple-choice rerun values are written option|confidence in one string.
- Raw intent labels resolve under the taxonomy version of the rerun's harness.
- Result rows in rerun_results.jsonl are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- When rerun_results.jsonl holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- rerun_results.jsonl timestamps are UTC.

results_fleet_a.log:
- Lines of results_fleet_a.log whose timestamp ends in Z were written by the uploader in UTC with a corrected clock; they take no zone or clock correction.
- Intent labels in results_fleet_a.log are raw aliases; the same alias can resolve to different classes under taxonomy v1 and v2, chosen by the job's harness version.
- Text values in results_fleet_a.log that contain spaces or equals signs are double-quoted; a quoted value is one field.
- Zone and clock settings for results_fleet_a.log rows follow the inventory snapshot of the submission week, not the host's first or most common snapshot; re-imaged hosts change zone mid-quarter.
- Result rows in results_fleet_a.log are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- Numbers in results_fleet_a.log follow the host locale of the submission week; de_DE hosts write a comma decimal mark and dot thousands grouping.
- Numbers in results_fleet_a.log follow the host locale of the submission week; fr_FR hosts write a comma decimal mark and space thousands grouping.
- Numbers in results_fleet_a.log follow the host locale of the submission week; en_IN hosts write Indian lakh grouping (12,34,567.50).
- Negative numbers in results_fleet_a.log can be written in parentheses.
- When results_fleet_a.log holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- A REVERSE line in results_fleet_a.log cancels the row whose id it names; the cancelled row and the REVERSE line itself carry no result.
- In results_fleet_a.log an arithmetic answer of N/A is an abstention and scores as wrong; it is not a missing value.
- Host-written timestamps in results_fleet_a.log carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log for a job whose routing entry names neither results_fleet_a.log as primary nor as copy source are stray and are ignored.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_a.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Europe/Berlin the offset during the window is UTC+02:00.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_a.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Chicago the offset during the window is UTC-05:00.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_a.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Asia/Kolkata the offset during the window is UTC+05:30.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_a.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Sao_Paulo the offset during the window is UTC-03:00.

results_fleet_b.log:
- Lines of results_fleet_b.log whose timestamp ends in Z were written by the uploader in UTC with a corrected clock; they take no zone or clock correction.
- Zone and clock settings for results_fleet_b.log rows follow the inventory snapshot of the submission week, not the host's first or most common snapshot; re-imaged hosts change zone mid-quarter.
- Result rows in results_fleet_b.log are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- Numbers in results_fleet_b.log follow the host locale of the submission week; de_DE hosts write a comma decimal mark and dot thousands grouping.
- Numbers in results_fleet_b.log follow the host locale of the submission week; fr_FR hosts write a comma decimal mark and space thousands grouping.
- Numbers in results_fleet_b.log follow the host locale of the submission week; en_IN hosts write Indian lakh grouping (12,34,567.50).
- When results_fleet_b.log holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- A REVERSE line in results_fleet_b.log cancels the row whose id it names; the cancelled row and the REVERSE line itself carry no result.
- In results_fleet_b.log, hosts whose submission-week inventory scale is pct write scores and confidences as percentages.
- Host-written timestamps in results_fleet_b.log carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.
- Rows of results_fleet_b.log for a job whose routing entry names neither results_fleet_b.log as primary nor as copy source are stray and are ignored.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_b.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Europe/Berlin the offset during the window is UTC+02:00.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_b.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Chicago the offset during the window is UTC-05:00.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_b.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Asia/Kolkata the offset during the window is UTC+05:30.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_b.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Sao_Paulo the offset during the window is UTC-03:00.

results_fleet_c.log:
- Lines of results_fleet_c.log whose timestamp ends in Z were written by the uploader in UTC with a corrected clock; they take no zone or clock correction.
- Text values in results_fleet_c.log that contain spaces or equals signs are double-quoted; a quoted value is one field.
- Zone and clock settings for results_fleet_c.log rows follow the inventory snapshot of the submission week, not the host's first or most common snapshot; re-imaged hosts change zone mid-quarter.
- Result rows in results_fleet_c.log are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- Some results_fleet_c.log lines were relayed as Latin-1 bytes; a line that is not valid UTF-8 is Latin-1.
- When results_fleet_c.log holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- A REVERSE line in results_fleet_c.log cancels the row whose id it names; the cancelled row and the REVERSE line itself carry no result.
- Host-written timestamps in results_fleet_c.log carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.
- Rows of results_fleet_c.log for a job whose routing entry names neither results_fleet_c.log as primary nor as copy source are stray and are ignored.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_c.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Europe/Berlin the offset during the window is UTC+02:00.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_c.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Chicago the offset during the window is UTC-05:00.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_c.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is Asia/Kolkata the offset during the window is UTC+05:30.
- Timestamps in results_fleet_c.log without an explicit zone are host wall-clock time; for jobs whose submission-week inventory zone is America/Sao_Paulo the offset during the window is UTC-03:00.

review_minutes.docx:
- Some cells in review_minutes.docx tables are wrapped in double quotes; the quotes are not part of the value.
- Text cells inside review_minutes.docx can end with a carriage return and line feed; strip them.
- The first cell of some review_minutes.docx table rows ends with a no-break space.
- Revision values in review_minutes.docx can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in review_minutes.docx can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Cells of review_minutes.docx can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

run_registry.xml:
- Attribute values use XML entities.
- Entries written after the schema migration use the element run with attributes alias and ckpt instead of job, model and checkpoint.
- A job registered twice keeps the entry with the higher seq.
- The registered host decides staging status through the host inventory week entry.

scores_shard1.parquet:
- num values in scores_shard1.parquet that look like integers (for example 1.0) are not percentages unless the host-week scale says so.
- num is -1.0 on rows of text suites (intent, multiling) because they have no numeric field; it is not a missing score.
- Result rows in scores_shard1.parquet are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- When scores_shard1.parquet holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- In scores_shard1.parquet, the row groups listed for it in the source conventions store emitted as epoch seconds; all other row groups store epoch milliseconds.
- In scores_shard1.parquet, rows from hosts whose submission-week inventory scale is pct carry num as a percentage.
- Host-written timestamps in scores_shard1.parquet carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet for a job whose routing entry names neither scores_shard1.parquet as primary nor as copy source are stray and are ignored.

scores_shard2.parquet:
- num values in scores_shard2.parquet that look like integers (for example 1.0) are not percentages unless the host-week scale says so.
- num is -1.0 on rows of text suites (intent, multiling) because they have no numeric field; it is not a missing score.
- Intent labels in scores_shard2.parquet are raw aliases resolved under the taxonomy version of the job's harness.
- Result rows in scores_shard2.parquet are provisional until the job is sealed; a row emitted after the seal time in the operations mailbox is ignored.
- When scores_shard2.parquet holds more than one live row for the same job, item and sample, the row with the latest emitted instant (UTC) wins.
- In scores_shard2.parquet, the row groups listed for it in the source conventions store emitted as epoch seconds; all other row groups store epoch milliseconds.
- In scores_shard2.parquet, rows from hosts whose submission-week inventory scale is pct carry num as a percentage.
- Host-written timestamps in scores_shard2.parquet carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.
- Rows of scores_shard2.parquet for a job whose routing entry names neither scores_shard2.parquet as primary nor as copy source are stray and are ignored.

scores_staging.parquet:
- num values in scores_staging.parquet that look like integers (for example 1.0) are not percentages unless the host-week scale says so.
- num is -1.0 on rows of text suites (intent, multiling) because they have no numeric field; it is not a missing score.
- Intent labels in scores_staging.parquet are raw aliases resolved under the taxonomy version of the job's harness.
- In scores_staging.parquet, the row groups listed for it in the source conventions store emitted as epoch seconds; all other row groups store epoch milliseconds.
- In scores_staging.parquet, rows from hosts whose submission-week inventory scale is pct carry num as a percentage.
- Host-written timestamps in scores_staging.parquet carry the host clock error for the submission week; subtract the clock offset listed for that host and week.
- Whether a staging row's job ran on a staging host is decided by the host inventory week entry, not by the export it sits in.

scoring_parameters.xlsx:
- Text cells inside scoring_parameters.xlsx can end with a carriage return and line feed; strip them.
- When scoring_parameters.xlsx holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in scoring_parameters.xlsx can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in scoring_parameters.xlsx can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).

slice_attributes.csv:
- A three-letter upper-case domain is a legacy code only when it is one of FIN, LAW, HLT, SCI, TEC, EVD; other upper-case values are just upper-cased domains.
- Attribute values can be in any letter case.
- Difficulty can be written as 1, 2 or 3 (easy, medium, hard).
- Difficulty can be written simple, moderate or difficult (easy, medium, hard).
- Domain values can be legacy codes (FIN, LAW, HLT, SCI, TEC, EVD) used before revision 3; slice_definitions.yaml maps them.
- Domain values can be upper-cased.
- An empty difficulty means medium.
- A repeated header inside slice_attributes.csv reorders its columns for the rows after it.
- When slice_attributes.csv holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in slice_attributes.csv can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in slice_attributes.csv can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Cells of slice_attributes.csv can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

slice_definitions.yaml:
- A # after an entry starts a comment.
- Some region entries are written in block style over three lines instead of flow style.
- Some region entries name the item key id instead of item.
- Region values can be written as long names (Europe, North America, Asia-Pacific) or sales codes (EMEA-EU, AMER-N, AMER-S, EMEA-ME); the region_aliases map resolves them.
- When slice_definitions.yaml holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- Revision values in slice_definitions.yaml can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in slice_definitions.yaml can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Some strings use single quotes.

suite_manifest.xlsx:
- Some changed_at cells are ISO text dates instead of serial numbers.
- Text cells inside suite_manifest.xlsx can end with a carriage return and line feed; strip them.
- When suite_manifest.xlsx holds several entries for one item id, the entry with the highest revision wins.
- changed_at is a spreadsheet serial date (days since 1899-12-30, fractional days are time of day UTC).
- Each sheet orders its columns differently; read the header row of each sheet.
- Revision cells can be text instead of numbers.
